# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 3 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high |

| dc_low | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_low |
| dc_low | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_low |
| dc_mid | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_mid |
| dc_mid | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_mid |
| dc_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_high |
| dc_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_high |
| light_heavy | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | light_heavy |

| psrr_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | psrr_high |
| psrr_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | psrr_high |

Defaults and seed requirements:

- nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_low: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_mid: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_high: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- light_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- mid_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- burst: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- line: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_low: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_high: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.

The domain is a grid of rectangles per polarity and process section. I'll summarize the grid boundaries and check whether the tt/ss/ff sections differ.

ACTION
0:00:32
Bash

Summarize geometry grid bounds per polarity and section

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
lines = open('devices.md', encoding='utf-8').read().split('\n')
d = json.loads(lines[15])
for pol in ['n','p']:
    secs = d[pol]
    print('=== polarity', pol)
    for sec in ['tt','ss','ff']:
        rects = secs[sec]
        ws = sorted(set([r['wmin'] for r in rects] + [r['wmax'] for r in rects]))
        ls = sorted(set([r['lmin'] for r in rects] + [r['lmax'] for r in rects]))
        print(sec, 'nrects', len(rects), 'keys', set(tuple(sorted(r.keys())) for r in rects))
        print('  W bounds', ws)
        print('  L bounds', ls)
    # compare sections
    sets = {sec: set((r['lmin'], r['lmax'], r['wmin'], r['wmax']) for r in secs[sec]) for sec in ['tt','ss','ff']}
    print('tt==ss', sets['tt']==sets['ss'], 'tt==ff', sets['tt']==sets['ff'], 'ss==ff', sets['ss']==sets['ff'])
    print('diff tt-ss', sorted(sets['tt']-sets['ss'])[:10], 'ss-tt', sorted(sets['ss']-sets['tt'])[:10])
    print('diff tt-ff', sorted(sets['tt']-sets['ff'])[:10], 'ff-tt', sorted(sets['ff']-sets['tt'])[:10])
EOF

0:00:34

All three process sections are identical for each polarity, so the domain is one grid per polarity. Next I'll list which W ranges are admissible for each L range.

ACTION
0:00:35
Bash

List admissible W intervals per L cell for each polarity

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
lines = open('devices.md', encoding='utf-8').read().split('\n')
d = json.loads(lines[15])
for pol in ['n','p']:
    rects = d[pol]['tt']
    print('=== polarity', pol, '(admissible W intervals per L cell; W<=100, L>=0.15)')
    Lcells = sorted(set((r['lmin'], r['lmax']) for r in rects))
    for (lmin, lmax) in Lcells:
        ws = sorted((r['wmin'], r['wmax']) for r in rects if r['lmin']==lmin and r['lmax']==lmax)
        # merge contiguous
        merged = []
        for a,b in ws:
            if merged and abs(merged[-1][1]-a) < 1e-12:
                merged[-1][1] = b
            else:
                merged.append([a,b])
        print(f'L[{lmin},{lmax}]:', ', '.join(f'[{a},{b}]' for a,b in merged))
EOF

0:05:14

Key finding for v1 at nominal/200 pF: DC regulation is fine, but the load steps give about 265 mV droop and 168 mV overshoot against an 80 mV limit, with recovery around 9 µs against 5 µs. The Miller capacitor is multiplied by the pass gain at light load, which slows the gate, so I'll sweep compensation and bias values in one batch and summarize each variant against the spec.

ACTION
0:05:19
Bash

Sweep compensation and bias variants via client measurements

mkdir -p /tmp/sweep && cat > /tmp/sweep/gen.py <<'EOF'
def netlist(cc="5p", rz="20k", rb="200k", wp="70", wn="20", ln="0.5", tail_w="16"):
    return f"""* LDO sweep variant
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RF1 VOUT FB 1meg
RF2 FB VSS 1meg
RB VIN BIAS {rb}
XM6 BIAS BIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=2 L=1 nf=1 m=1
XM5 TAIL BIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={tail_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XM1 ND1 FB TAIL VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wn} L={ln} nf=1 m=1
XM2 N1 VREF TAIL VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wn} L={ln} nf=1 m=1
XM3 ND1 ND1 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XM4 N1 ND1 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XMP VOUT N1 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
RZ N1 CX {rz}
CC CX VOUT {cc}
.ends DUT
"""
EOF
cat > /tmp/sweep/run.py <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess, sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from gen import netlist

def run_variant(tag, exp_id, pvt='N', cl=None, **kw):
    with open('/workspace/dut.spice', 'w') as f:
        f.write(netlist(**kw))
    req = {"action": "measure", "experiment_id": exp_id, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt}
    if cl:
        req["cl"] = cl
    rp = f'/tmp/sweep/req_{exp_id}.json'
    with open(rp, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(req, f)
    out = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], cwd='/workspace', capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out.stdout)
    except Exception:
        print(tag, 'PARSE_FAIL', out.stdout[:300], out.stderr[:300])
        return None
    with open(f'/tmp/sweep/res_{exp_id}.json', 'w') as f:
        json.dump(d, f)
    return d

def summarize(d):
    if d is None:
        return 'NONE'
    res = {}
    for c in d.get('cases', []):
        m = c.get('measurements', {})
        cid = c['case_id']
        if cid.startswith('dc'):
            res[cid] = f"v={m.get('vout_v', float('nan')):.4f} ovh={m.get('overhead_a', 0)*1e6:.1f}u"
        else:
            env = m.get('envelope', {})
            wins = m.get('recovery_windows', [])
            rec = [round(w.get('recovery_s', -1)*1e6, 2) for w in wins]
            res[cid] = f"dr={env.get('droop_v', float('nan')):.3f} ov={env.get('overshoot_v', float('nan')):.3f} rec_us={rec}"
    return ' | '.join(f"{k}:{v}" for k, v in res.items())

if __name__ == '__main__':
    import itertools
    variants = []
    i = 0
    for cc, rz, rb in itertools.product(["0.2p", "0.5p", "1p", "2p", "5p"], ["1", "10k", "50k"], ["200k", "80k"]):
        i += 1
        variants.append((f"v{i:02d}", dict(cc=cc, rz=rz, rb=rb)))
    for tag, kw in variants:
        d = run_variant(tag, f"s_{tag}", **kw)
        print(tag, kw, summarize(d), flush=True)
EOF
cd /tmp/sweep && timeout 580 python3 run.py > /tmp/sweep/out1.txt 2>&1; echo exit=$?; cat /tmp/sweep/out1.txt | cut -c1-600

MODEL
0:13:02

The sweep shows droop barely changes with compensation or tail current, and the overhead is only about 4.8 µA, so the error amplifier is not conducting its tail current. The NMOS input pair sits at a 0.5 V common mode with a tail node near 0.5 V, which leaves it near cutoff. I'll switch to a PMOS input pair with an NMOS mirror load, bias it from a resistor to VSS, and re-sweep the compensation.

0:13:07
Bash

Sweep PMOS-input error amp variants via client

cat > /tmp/sweep/gen2.py <<'EOF'
def netlist(cc="1p", rz="20k", rbp="200k", wp="70", win="40", lin="0.15", wm="10", lm="0.5", tail_w="16"):
    return f"""* LDO v2: PMOS-input error amp, NMOS mirror, PMOS pass device
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RF1 VOUT FB 1meg
RF2 FB VSS 1meg
RBP BP VSS {rbp}
XMP6 BP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=2 L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP5 TAILP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={tail_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP1 ND1 FB TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMP2 N1 VREF TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMN3 ND1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMN4 N1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMP VOUT N1 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
RZ N1 CX {rz}
CC CX VOUT {cc}
.ends DUT
"""
EOF
cat > /tmp/sweep/run2.py <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess, sys, itertools
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from gen2 import netlist

def run_variant(exp_id, pvt='N', **kw):
    with open('/workspace/dut.spice', 'w') as f:
        f.write(netlist(**kw))
    req = {"action": "measure", "experiment_id": exp_id, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt}
    rp = f'/tmp/sweep/req_{exp_id}.json'
    with open(rp, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(req, f)
    out = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], cwd='/workspace', capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out.stdout)
    except Exception:
        return {'error': out.stdout[:300] + out.stderr[:300]}
    with open(f'/tmp/sweep/res_{exp_id}.json', 'w') as f:
        json.dump(d, f)
    return d

def fmt(x):
    return 'None' if x is None else f'{x:.3f}'

def summarize(d):
    if 'error' in d:
        return 'ERR ' + d['error']
    res = []
    for c in d.get('cases', []):
        m = c.get('measurements', {}) or {}
        cid = c['case_id']
        if cid.startswith('dc'):
            res.append(f"{cid}:v={m.get('vout_v', 0):.4f},ovh={m.get('overhead_a', 0)*1e6:.1f}u")
        else:
            env = m.get('envelope') or {}
            wins = m.get('recovery_windows') or []
            rec = [None if w.get('recovery_s') is None else round(w['recovery_s']*1e6, 2) for w in wins]
            res.append(f"{cid}:dr={fmt(env.get('droop_v'))},ov={fmt(env.get('overshoot_v'))},rec={rec}")
    return ' | '.join(res)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    variants = []
    for i, (cc, rz) in enumerate(itertools.product(["0.5p", "1p", "2p", "5p"], ["1", "20k"]), 1):
        variants.append((f"p{i:02d}", dict(cc=cc, rz=rz)))
    for tag, kw in variants:
        d = run_variant(f"s2_{tag}", **kw)
        print(tag, kw, summarize(d), flush=True)
EOF

The PMOS-input version fixes the overshoot (down to about 40 mV) but now has an 11% DC error at 1 mA and still about 220 mV droop. I'll sweep tail current, input and mirror lengths, and compensation in one batch, ranked by worst DC error, droop, and recovery.

Sweep tail, input length, mirror length and compensation

cat > /tmp/sweep/run3.py <<'EOF'
import sys, itertools, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run2 import run_variant

def metrics(d):
    if 'error' in d:
        return None
    dc = []; tr = []; rec = []
    for c in d.get('cases', []):
        m = c.get('measurements', {}) or {}
        cid = c['case_id']
        if cid.startswith('dc'):
            dc.append(abs(m.get('vout_v', 0) - 1.0))
        else:
            env = m.get('envelope') or {}
            tr.append(max(env.get('droop_v') or 0, env.get('overshoot_v') or 0))
            for w in (m.get('recovery_windows') or []):
                r = w.get('recovery_s')
                rec.append(math.inf if r is None else r)
    return max(dc), max(tr), max(rec) if rec else 0.0

if __name__ == '__main__':
    combos = list(itertools.product(["1p", "3p"], ["16", "64"], ["200k", "50k"], ["0.15", "0.5"], ["0.5", "1"]))
    results = []
    for i, (cc, tw, rbp, lin, lm) in enumerate(combos, 1):
        kw = dict(cc=cc, rz="20k", tail_w=tw, rbp=rbp, lin=lin, lm=lm)
        d = run_variant(f"s3_{i:02d}", **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)
        results.append((kw, mt))
        if mt is None:
            print(i, kw, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True)
        else:
            print(i, kw, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} tr={mt[1]:.4f} rec_us={mt[2]*1e6 if mt[2]!=math.inf else 'inf'}", flush=True)
EOF

MODEL
0:19:28

There is no local ngspice or PDK, so the client is the only simulator. Analysis suggests the single-stage error amp can't be both fast and stable, because its output resistance times the pass-gate capacitance is too large. I'll try a version with a small CMOS inverter buffer driving the pass gate, with the error-amp inputs swapped so the overall loop stays negative feedback.

ACTION
0:19:32
Bash

Sweep inverter-buffer LDO variants via client

cat > /tmp/sweep/gen3.py <<'EOF'
def netlist(cc="1p", rz="20k", rbp="200k", wp="70", win="40", lin="0.5", wm="10", lm="0.5", tail_w="16", wpi="4", wni="2", li="0.15"):
    return f"""* LDO v3: PMOS-input error amp with inverter buffer driving pass gate
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RF1 VOUT FB 1meg
RF2 FB VSS 1meg
RBP BP VSS {rbp}
XMP6 BP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=2 L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP5 TAILP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={tail_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP1 ND1 VREF TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMP2 N1 FB TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMN3 ND1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMN4 N1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMIP G N1 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wpi} L={li} nf=1 m=1
XMIN G N1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wni} L={li} nf=1 m=1
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
RZ N1 CX {rz}
CC CX VOUT {cc}
.ends DUT
"""
EOF
cat > /tmp/sweep/run4.py <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess, sys, itertools, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run3 import metrics
import importlib

def run_variant(gen_module, exp_id, pvt='N', **kw):
    gen = importlib.import_module(gen_module)
    with open('/workspace/dut.spice', 'w') as f:
        f.write(gen.netlist(**kw))
    req = {"action": "measure", "experiment_id": exp_id, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt}
    rp = f'/tmp/sweep/req_{exp_id}.json'
    with open(rp, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(req, f)
    out = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], cwd='/workspace', capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out.stdout)
    except Exception:
        return {'error': out.stdout[:300] + out.stderr[:300]}
    with open(f'/tmp/sweep/res_{exp_id}.json', 'w') as f:
        json.dump(d, f)
    return d

if __name__ == '__main__':
    combos = list(itertools.product(["0.2p", "1p"], ["0.5", "0.15"], [("4", "2"), ("12", "6")]))
    for i, (cc, lin, (wpi, wni)) in enumerate(combos, 1):
        kw = dict(cc=cc, lin=lin, wpi=wpi, wni=wni, rz="20k")
        d = run_variant('gen3', f"s4_{i:02d}", **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)
        if mt is None:
            print(i, kw, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True)
        else:
            rec = mt[2]
            print(i, kw, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} tr={mt[1]:.4f} rec_us={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6,3)}", flush=True)
EOF
cd /tmp/sweep && timeout 580 python3 run4.py > /tmp/sweep/out4.txt 2>&1; echo exit=$?; cut -c1-300 /tmp/sweep/out4.txt

MODEL
0:21:14

About 4925 s of model budget remain. The single-stage amplifier's output resistance (about 450 kΩ) times the pass-gate capacitance sets a roughly 45 ns response, so I'll add a small common-source NMOS buffer with a PMOS current-source pull-up. That should lower the gate impedance, and I'll sweep input length, tail, buffer width, and a small compensation cap.

sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run3 import metrics
import importlib

def run_variant(gen_module, exp_id, pvt='N', **kw):
    gen = importlib.import_module(gen_module)
    with open('/workspace/dut.spice', 'w') as f:
        f.write(gen.netlist(**kw))
    req = {"action": "measure", "experiment_id": exp_id, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt}
    rp = f'/tmp/sweep/req_{exp_id}.json'
    with open(rp, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(req, f)
    out = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], cwd='/workspace', capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out.stdout)
    except Exception:
        return {'error': out.stdout[:300] + out.stderr[:300]}
    with open(f'/tmp/sweep/res_{exp_id}.json', 'w') as f:
        json.dump(d, f)
    return d

if __name__ == '__main__':
    combos = list(itertools.product(["0.2p", "1p"], ["0.5", "0.15"], [("4", "2"), ("12", "6")]))
    for i, (cc, lin, (wpi, wni)) in enumerate(combos, 1):
        kw = dict(cc=cc, lin=lin, wpi=wpi, wni=wni, rz="20k")
        d = run_variant('gen3', f"s4_{i:02d}", **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)

def show(o,ind=0,depth=0):
    if depth>3: return
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        for k,v in list(o.items())[:40]:
            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)):
                print(' '*ind+str(k)+':', type(v).__name__, len(v))
                show(v,ind+2,depth+1)
            else:
                print(' '*ind+str(k)+':', str(v)[:120])
    elif isinstance(o,list) and o:
        show(o[0],ind+2,depth+1)

def netlist(cc="0.2p", rz="20k", rbp="200k", wp="70", win="40", lin="0.5", wm="10", lm="0.5", tail_w="16", pu_w="16", wb="4", lb="0.15"):
    return f"""* LDO v4: PMOS-input error amp, NMOS common-source buffer, PMOS pull-up source
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RF1 VOUT FB 1meg
RF2 FB VSS 1meg
RBP BP VSS {rbp}
XMP6 BP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=2 L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP5 TAILP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={tail_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP1 ND1 VREF TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMP2 N1 FB TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMN3 ND1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMN4 N1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMPU G BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={pu_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMB G N1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wb} L={lb} nf=1 m=1
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
RZ N1 CX {rz}
CC CX VOUT {cc}
.ends DUT
"""
---RUN3---
import sys, itertools, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run2 import run_variant

def metrics(d):
    if 'error' in d:
        return None
    dc = []; tr = []; rec = []
    for c in d.get('cases', []):
        m = c.get('measurements', {}) or {}
        cid = c['case_id']
        if cid.startswith('dc'):
            dc.append(abs(m.get('vout_v', 0) - 1.0))
        else:
            env = m.get('envelope') or {}
            tr.append(max(env.get('droop_v') or 0, env.get('overshoot_v') or 0))
            for w in (m.get('recovery_windows') or []):
                r = w.get('recovery_s')
                rec.append(math.inf if r is None else r)
    return max(dc), max(tr), max(rec) if rec else 0.0

if __name__ == '__main__':
    combos = list(itertools.product(["1p", "3p"], ["16", "64"], ["200k", "50k"], ["0.15", "0.5"], ["0.5", "1"]))
    results = []
    for i, (cc, tw, rbp, lin, lm) in enumerate(combos, 1):
        kw = dict(cc=cc, rz="20k", tail_w=tw, rbp=rbp, lin=lin, lm=lm)
        d = run_variant(f"s3_{i:02d}", **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)
        results.append((kw, mt))
        if mt is None:
            print(i, kw, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True)
        else:
            print(i, kw, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} tr={mt[1]:.4f} rec_us={mt[2]*1e6 if mt[2]!=math.inf else 'inf'}", flush=True)

The spec table shows the transient failures are about 0.12 V envelope error plus recovery never settling in the first window. I'll widen the sweep to the feedback divider impedance (1 MΩ / 1 MΩ gives a slow FB pole), tail, EA width, buffer, pull-up, and compensation, and run it in the background since sweep wall-time doesn't count against the model budget.

content
import sys, itertools, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run4 import run_variant
from run3 import metrics

combos = list(itertools.product(["100k", "50k"], ["64", "128"], ["64", "128"], ["10", "20"], ["20", "40"]))
for i, (rf, tw, pw, wb, win) in enumerate(combos, 1):
    kw = dict(rf=rf, lin="0.15", tail_w=tw, win=win, wb=wb, pu_w=pw, cc="1f")
    d = run_variant('gen5', f"s8_{i:02d}", **kw)
    mt = metrics(d)
    if mt is None:
        print(i, kw, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True)
    else:
        rec = mt[2]
        print(i, kw, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} tr={mt[1]:.4f} rec_us={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6, 3)}", flush=True)

List spec metrics and validate the current netlist

python3 -c "
import json
s=json.load(open('spec.json'))
def walk(o,p=''):
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        if 'metric' in o or 'limit' in o:
            print(p, {k:o.get(k) for k in ('metric','limit','direction','unit','analysis','case_id','load','cload_f','pvt','draft_standard_target') if k in o})
        for k,v in o.items(): walk(v,p+'/'+str(k))
    elif isinstance(o,list):
        for i,v in enumerate(o): walk(v,p+'['+str(i)+']')
walk(s)
print(list(s.keys()) if isinstance(s,dict) else type(s))
" | cut -c1-300 | head -80; echo; atb-client validate 2>&1 | head -c 600

import json, subprocess, sys, math
PVTS = ["N", "SLC", "SLH", "SHC", "SHH", "FLC", "FLH", "FHC", "FHH"]
CLS = [("2e-10", 2e-10), ("5e-10", 5e-10)]
for cl_tag, cl in CLS:
    for pvt in PVTS:
        eid = f"v9_{pvt}_{cl_tag.replace('-', 'm').replace('.', '')}"
        rp = f"/tmp/sweep/req_{eid}.json"
        with open(rp, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt, "cl": cl}, f)
        out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", rp], cwd="/workspace", capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
        try:
            d = json.loads(out.stdout)
        except Exception:
            print(eid, "PARSE_ERR", out.stdout[:200], out.stderr[:200], flush=True)
            continue
        with open(f"/tmp/sweep/res_{eid}.json", "w") as f:
            json.dump(d, f)
        if not d.get("ok", False):
            print(eid, "NOT_OK", str(d)[:300], flush=True)
            continue
        rows = d.get("summary", {}).get("spec_table", [])
        miss = [f"{r['case_id']}:{r['metric']}={r['measured']:.4g}(lim {r['limit']})" for r in rows if r.get("verdict") != "PASS"]
        worst = {}
        for r in rows:
            k = r["metric"]
            worst[k] = max(worst.get(k, -math.inf), r["measured"] if r["measured"] is not None else math.inf)
        print(eid, "timed_out" if d.get("timed_out") else "", " ".join(f"{k}={v:.4g}" for k, v in worst.items() if k in ("dc_error_v", "overhead_a", "envelope_error_v", "recovery_0_s", "recovery_1_s", "vref_abs_a", "initial_hold_error_v")), "MISS:" + ";".join(miss) if miss else "ALL_PASS", flush=True)

def netlist(rf="50k", rbp="200k", w5="16", wt="100", wn="100", win="20", lin="0.15", wl="40", ll="0.5", pu_w="64", wb="20", lb="0.15", wp="70"):

import sys, itertools, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run4 import run_variant
from run3 import metrics

def overhead(d):
    vals = [(c.get('measurements') or {}).get('overhead_a') for c in d.get('cases', []) if c['case_id'].startswith('dc')]
    vals = [v for v in vals if v is not None]
    return max(vals) if vals else float('nan')

combos = list(itertools.product(["8", "16"], ["10", "20", "40"], ["10", "20"], ["32", "64"], ["50k", "100k"]))
for i, (w5, win, wb, pw, rf) in enumerate(combos, 1):
    kw = dict(w5=w5, win=win, wb=wb, pu_w=pw, rf=rf)
    d = run_variant('gen6', f"s10_{i:02d}", **kw)
    mt = metrics(d)
    if mt is None:
        print(i, kw, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True)
    else:
        rec = mt[2]
        print(i, kw, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} tr={mt[1]:.4f} rec_us={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6, 3)} oh_uA={overhead(d)*1e6:.1f}", flush=True)

The NMOS-input amp is worse at nominal (transients 0.10–0.19 V, recovery never settles), so I'm dropping it and returning to the PMOS-input design, which passes most corners. The SLC failure comes from the tail gate bias: its Vsg equals the bias diode's Vsg, which is high because that diode is narrow (W=2). I'll sweep a wider bias diode and lower bias resistor at the SLC, FHH, and nominal corners.

# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.
----
# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.
----
{

/usr/local/bin/atb-client
# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|

| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 3 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high |

import json, subprocess, sys, itertools, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run3 import metrics
import importlib

def run_variant(gen_module, exp_id, pvt='N', **kw):
    gen = importlib.import_module(gen_module)
    with open('/workspace/dut.spice', 'w') as f:
        f.write(gen.netlist(**kw))
    req = {"action": "measure", "experiment_id": exp_id, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt}
    rp = f'/tmp/sweep/req_{exp_id}.json'
    with open(rp, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(req, f)
    out = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], cwd='/workspace', capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out.stdout)
    except Exception:
        return {'error': out.stdout[:300] + out.stderr[:300]}
    with open(f'/tmp/sweep/res_{exp_id}.json', 'w') as f:
        json.dump(d, f)
    return d

if __name__ == '__main__':
    combos = list(itertools.product(["0.2p", "1p"], ["0.5", "0.15"], [("4", "2"), ("12", "6")]))
    for i, (cc, lin, (wpi, wni)) in enumerate(combos, 1):
        kw = dict(cc=cc, lin=lin, wpi=wpi, wni=wni, rz="20k")
        d = run_variant('gen3', f"s4_{i:02d}", **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)
        if mt is None:
            print(i, kw, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True)
        else:
            rec = mt[2]
            print(i, kw, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} tr={mt[1]:.4f} rec_us={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6,3)}", flush=True)
----
import sys, itertools, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run2 import run_variant

def metrics(d):
    if 'error' in d:
        return None
    dc = []; tr = []; rec = []
    for c in d.get('cases', []):
        m = c.get('measurements', {}) or {}
        cid = c['case_id']
        if cid.startswith('dc'):
            dc.append(abs(m.get('vout_v', 0) - 1.0))
        else:
            env = m.get('envelope') or {}
            tr.append(max(env.get('droop_v') or 0, env.get('overshoot_v') or 0))
            for w in (m.get('recovery_windows') or []):
                r = w.get('recovery_s')
                rec.append(math.inf if r is None else r)
    return max(dc), max(tr), max(rec) if rec else 0.0

if __name__ == '__main__':
    combos = list(itertools.product(["1p", "3p"], ["16", "64"], ["200k", "50k"], ["0.15", "0.5"], ["0.5", "1"]))
    results = []
    for i, (cc, tw, rbp, lin, lm) in enumerate(combos, 1):
        kw = dict(cc=cc, rz="20k", tail_w=tw, rbp=rbp, lin=lin, lm=lm)
        d = run_variant(f"s3_{i:02d}", **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)
        results.append((kw, mt))
        if mt is None:
            print(i, kw, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True)
        else:
            print(i, kw, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} tr={mt[1]:.4f} rec_us={mt[2]*1e6 if mt[2]!=math.inf else 'inf'}", flush=True)
----
def netlist(cc="0.2p", rz="20k", rbp="200k", wp="70", win="40", lin="0.15", wm="10", lm="0.5", tail_w="16", pu_w="16", wb="4", lb="0.15", rf="1meg"):
    return f"""* LDO v5: PMOS-input error amp, NMOS common-source buffer, PMOS pull-up source
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RF1 VOUT FB {rf}
RF2 FB VSS {rf}
RBP BP VSS {rbp}
XMP6 BP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=2 L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP5 TAILP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={tail_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP1 ND1 VREF TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMP2 N1 FB TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMN3 ND1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMN4 N1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMPU G BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={pu_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMB G N1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wb} L={lb} nf=1 m=1
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
RZ N1 CX {rz}
CC CX VOUT {cc}
.ends DUT
"""

import json, subprocess, sys, itertools, importlib
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')

def run_an(gen_module, exp_id, analysis, pvt, **kw):
    gen = importlib.import_module(gen_module)
    with open('/workspace/dut.spice', 'w') as f:
        f.write(gen.netlist(**kw))
    rp = f'/tmp/sweep/req_{exp_id}.json'
    with open(rp, 'w') as f:
        json.dump({"action": "measure", "experiment_id": exp_id, "analysis": analysis, "pvt": pvt}, f)
    out = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], cwd='/workspace', capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out.stdout)
    except Exception:
        return {'error': out.stdout[:300] + out.stderr[:300]}
    with open(f'/tmp/sweep/res_{exp_id}.json', 'w') as f:
        json.dump(d, f)
    return d

if __name__ == '__main__':
    combos = list(itertools.product(["20", "100"], ["64", "8"], ["70", "100"]))
    for i, (wb, pw, wp) in enumerate(combos, 1):
        kw = dict(wb=wb, pu_w=pw, wp=wp)
        d = run_an('gen7', f"s13_{i:02d}", 'dc_high', 'SLC', **kw)
        if 'error' in d:
            print(i, kw, 'ERR', d['error'][:200], flush=True); continue
        vals = [(c['case_id'], (c.get('measurements') or {}).get('vout_v')) for c in d.get('cases', [])]
        print(i, kw, vals, d.get('summary', '') if i == 1 else '', flush=True)

Run nominal verification of wider pass device across PVT corners

cat > /tmp/sweep/run14.py <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess, sys, math, importlib
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run3 import metrics

def run_req(gen_module, exp_id, analysis, pvt, cl=None, **kw):
    gen = importlib.import_module(gen_module)
    with open('/workspace/dut.spice', 'w') as f:
        f.write(gen.netlist(**kw))
    req = {"action": "measure", "experiment_id": exp_id, "analysis": analysis, "pvt": pvt}
    if cl is not None:
        req["cl"] = cl
    rp = f'/tmp/sweep/req_{exp_id}.json'
    with open(rp, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(req, f)
    out = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], cwd='/workspace', capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out.stdout)
    except Exception:
        return {'error': out.stdout[:300] + out.stderr[:300]}
    with open(f'/tmp/sweep/res_{exp_id}.json', 'w') as f:
        json.dump(d, f)
    return d

def overhead(d):
    vals = [(c.get('measurements') or {}).get('overhead_a') for c in d.get('cases', []) if c['case_id'].startswith('dc')]
    vals = [v for v in vals if v is not None]
    return max(vals) if vals else float('nan')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    tag = sys.argv[1]
    kw = json.loads(sys.argv[2])
    pvts = sys.argv[3].split(',')
    cl = float(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 2e-10
    for pvt in pvts:
        eid = f"s14{tag}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}"
        d = run_req('gen7', eid, 'nominal', pvt, cl=cl, **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)
        if mt is None:
            print(pvt, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True); continue
        rec = mt[2]
        print(pvt, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} env={mt[1]*1e3:.1f}mV rec_us={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6, 3)} oh_uA={overhead(d)*1e6:.1f}", flush=True)
EOF

def netlist(rf="50k", rbp="200k", w6="2", tail_w="64", win="20", lin="0.15", wm="10", lm="0.5", pu_w="64", wb="20", lb="0.15", wp="100", npass="1", cc="", rz="20k"):
    extra = "".join(f"XMPX{k} VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L=0.15 nf=1 m=1\n" for k in range(2, int(npass) + 1))
    comp = f"RZ N1 CX {rz}\nCC CX VOUT {cc}\n" if cc else ""

.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF

cat > /tmp/sweep/run17.py <<'EOF'
import json, sys, math, random
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run14 import run_req, overhead
from run3 import metrics
random.seed(int(sys.argv[1]))
space = dict(win=["20", "100"], lin=["0.15", "0.3"], wm=["10", "40"], wb=["20", "60"], pu_w=["16", "64"], npass=["1", "2"], tail_w=["64", "100"], cc=["", "1p", "5p"])
seen = set()
N = int(sys.argv[2])
i = 0
while i < N:
    kw = {k: random.choice(v) for k, v in space.items()}
    key = tuple(sorted(kw.items()))
    if key in seen:
        continue
    seen.add(key); i += 1
    d = run_req('gen9', f"s17_{sys.argv[1]}_{i:02d}", 'nominal', 'SLC', cl=2e-10, **kw)
    mt = metrics(d)
    if mt is None:
        print(i, kw, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:150], flush=True); continue
    vo = {c['case_id']: round((c.get('measurements') or {}).get('vout_v') or float('nan'), 3) for c in d['cases'] if c['case_id'].startswith('dc')}
    rec = mt[2]
    print(i, json.dumps(kw), vo, f"dc={mt[0]:.4f} env={mt[1]*1e3:.1f} rec={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6, 2)} oh={overhead(d)*1e6:.1f}", flush=True)
EOF

Run background random search over EA and output-stage sizing

cd /tmp/sweep && cat > run20.py <<'EOF'
import json, sys, math, random
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run14 import run_req, overhead
from run3 import metrics
seed, N = int(sys.argv[1]), int(sys.argv[2])
random.seed(seed)
space = dict(lm=["0.15", "0.3", "0.5"], wm=["5", "10", "20"], win=["20", "40"], lin=["0.15", "0.3"], pu_w=["32", "48", "64"], wb=["20", "30", "40"], rbp=["200k", "100k"], tail_w=["64", "100"])
corners = ["FLC", "SHC", "FHH"]
seen = set()
i = 0
while i < N:
    kw = {k: random.choice(v) for k, v in space.items()}
    key = tuple(sorted(kw.items()))
    if key in seen:
        continue
    seen.add(key); i += 1
    kw.update(npass="1", wp="100")
    res = []
    npass_ct = 0
    for pvt in corners:
        d = run_req('gen9', f"s20_{seed}_{i:02d}_{pvt}", 'nominal', pvt, cl=2e-10, **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)
        if mt is None:
            res.append((pvt, 'ERR')); continue
        rec = mt[2]
        ok = mt[0] <= 0.03 and mt[1] <= 0.08 and rec != math.inf and rec <= 5e-6
        npass_ct += ok
        res.append((pvt, round(mt[0]*100, 2), round(mt[1]*1e3, 1), 'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6, 2), 'P' if ok else 'F'))
    print(i, json.dumps({k: kw[k] for k in space}), npass_ct, res, flush=True)
EOF

# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |

# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

import json, subprocess, sys, math, importlib
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run3 import metrics

def run_req(gen_module, exp_id, analysis, pvt, cl=None, **kw):
    gen = importlib.import_module(gen_module)
    with open('/workspace/dut.spice', 'w') as f:
        f.write(gen.netlist(**kw))
    req = {"action": "measure", "experiment_id": exp_id, "analysis": analysis, "pvt": pvt}
    if cl is not None:
        req["cl"] = cl
    rp = f'/tmp/sweep/req_{exp_id}.json'
    with open(rp, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(req, f)
    out = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], cwd='/workspace', capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out.stdout)
    except Exception:
        return {'error': out.stdout[:300] + out.stderr[:300]}
    with open(f'/tmp/sweep/res_{exp_id}.json', 'w') as f:
        json.dump(d, f)
    return d

def overhead(d):
    vals = [(c.get('measurements') or {}).get('overhead_a') for c in d.get('cases', []) if c['case_id'].startswith('dc')]
    vals = [v for v in vals if v is not None]
    return max(vals) if vals else float('nan')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    tag = sys.argv[1]
    kw = json.loads(sys.argv[2])
    pvts = sys.argv[3].split(',')
    cl = float(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 2e-10
    for pvt in pvts:
        eid = f"s14{tag}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}"
        d = run_req('gen7', eid, 'nominal', pvt, cl=cl, **kw)
        mt = metrics(d)
        if mt is None:
            print(pvt, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:200], flush=True); continue
        rec = mt[2]
        print(pvt, f"dc_err={mt[0]:.4f} env={mt[1]*1e3:.1f}mV rec_us={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6, 3)} oh_uA={overhead(d)*1e6:.1f}", flush=True)

import json, sys, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run14 import run_req, overhead
from run3 import metrics
tag, cl = sys.argv[1], float(sys.argv[2])
kw = json.loads(sys.argv[3])
for pvt in sys.argv[4].split(','):
    d = run_req('gen9', f"s19{tag}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}", 'nominal', pvt, cl=cl, **kw)
    mt = metrics(d)
    if mt is None:
        print(pvt, 'ERR', d.get('error', '')[:150], flush=True); continue
    vo = {c['case_id']: round((c.get('measurements') or {}).get('vout_v') or float('nan'), 3) for c in d['cases'] if c['case_id'].startswith('dc')}
    rec = mt[2]
    print(pvt, vo, f"dc={mt[0]:.4f} env={mt[1]*1e3:.1f} rec={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6, 2)} oh={overhead(d)*1e6:.1f}", flush=True)

def netlist(rf="50k", rbp="200k", w6="2", tail_w="64", win="20", lin="0.15", wm="10", lm="0.5", pu_w="64", wb="20", lb="0.15", wp="100", npass="1", cc="", rz="20k", cg="", pu2=""):
    extra = "".join(f"XMPX{k} VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L=0.15 nf=1 m=1\n" for k in range(2, int(npass) + 1))
    comp = f"RZ N1 CX {rz}\nCC CX VOUT {cc}\n" if cc else ""
    comp += f"CG VOUT G {cg}\n" if cg else ""
    comp += f"XMPU2 G BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={pu2} L=1 nf=1 m=1\n" if pu2 else ""
    return f"""* LDO v9: PMOS-input error amp, NMOS common-source gate driver, PMOS pull-up current source, parallel pass devices
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RF1 VOUT FB {rf}
RF2 FB VSS {rf}
RBP BP VSS {rbp}
XMP6 BP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={w6} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP5 TAILP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={tail_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMP1 ND1 VREF TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMP2 N1 FB TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
XMN3 ND1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMN4 N1 ND1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wm} L={lm} nf=1 m=1
XMPU G BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={pu_w} L=1 nf=1 m=1
XMB G N1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wb} L={lb} nf=1 m=1
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
{extra}{comp}.ends DUT
"""

Bash

Summarize stored measurement results from earlier sweeps

cd /tmp/sweep && python3 - <<'EOF' 2>&1 | cut -c1-260
import json, glob, math, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/sweep')
from run3 import metrics
from run14 import overhead
rows = []
for fn in sorted(glob.glob('/tmp/sweep/res_s19*_200.json') + glob.glob('/tmp/sweep/res_s19*_500.json')):
    m = re.match(r'.*res_(s19(.*)_(FLC|FHH|FHC|FLH|SHC|SHH|SLC|SLH|N)_(200|500))\.json', fn)
    if not m: continue
    tag, pvt, cl = m.group(2), m.group(3), m.group(4)
    try:
        d = json.load(open(fn))
    except Exception:
        continue
    mt = metrics(d)
    if mt is None:
        rows.append((tag, pvt, cl, 'ERR')); continue
    vo = {c['case_id']: round((c.get('measurements') or {}).get('vout_v') or float('nan'), 3) for c in d['cases'] if c['case_id'].startswith('dc')}
    rec = mt[2]
    rows.append((tag, pvt, cl, [vo.get(k) for k in ('dc_low','dc_mid','dc_high')], f"dc={mt[0]:.4f} env={mt[1]*1e3:.1f} rec={'inf' if rec == math.inf else round(rec*1e6,2)} oh={overhead(d)*1e6:.1f}"))
for r in rows:
    print(*r)
EOF

Validation passes and about 3440 s of model time remains. Two pass devices still ring, and the likely cause is the doubled gate capacitance against the gate-node conductance. Bash

Submit the frozen design with an honest rationale

atb-client submit --rationale "Single-stage PMOS-pass LDO. PMOS-input error amplifier (XMP1/XMP2 gated by VREF and feedback, tail XMP5 from a diode-biased bias node BP through 200k) with NMOS mirror load (XMN3 diode and XMN4, both 10/0.15) driving an NMOS common-source gate driver XMB (20/0.15) against a PMOS current-source pull-up XMPU (64/1). Pass device XMP is 100/0.15 from VIN to VOUT. Feedback 50k/50k. Nominal measurements at 2e-10 and 5e-10: DC error, overhead and transient/recovery pass at N, SLH, SHH, FLH, FHC and FHH at both loads (max overhead 77 uA). SHC passes at 5e-10 but at 2e-10 recovery does not settle (DC error 1.7 percent, envelope 53 mV). NOT MET: SLC (1 mA output 0.905 V, DC error 9.5 percent, envelope 165-210 mV, unsettled) and FLC (1 mA output 0.953 V, DC error 4.7 percent, unsettled recovery) at both loads. Two parallel pass devices fixed the SLC 1 mA headroom but made the loop ring at N, SHC and FLC; mirror-width and pull-up trims shift all corners together and could not satisfy FLC and FHH at once. This submission does not meet every required check." 2>&1 | head -c 3000

MODEL
1:45:37

I submitted design L (revision dut-5a652b5e4f965156ea1694da) and the session is now closed. It does not meet every required check, so the submission is a partial result.

  • Passes at both 2e-10 and 5e-10 loads: N, SLH, SHH, FLH, FHC, FHH. SHC passes at 5e-10 only.
  • Fails at both loads: SLC (1 mA output 0.905 V, 9.5% DC error, 165–210 mV envelope, recovery never settles) and FLC (1 mA output 0.953 V, 4.7% DC error, recovery never settles).
  • Fails at 2e-10 only: SHC (1.7% DC error, 53 mV envelope, recovery never settles).

Design decisions:

  • Pass device: a 100/0.15 PMOS. An NMOS pass would need its gate above about 1.7 V, which is beyond the 1.35 V maximum input.
  • Error amplifier: PMOS input pair with an NMOS mirror load, mirror length 0.15 µm. Longer mirrors helped FLC but pushed FHH and SHH out of spec.
  • Output stage: NMOS common-source gate driver against a PMOS current-source pull-up. Overhead stays at or below 77 µA.

Two parallel pass devices fixed the SLC 1 mA headroom but made the loop ring at N, SHC and FLC. Mirror-width and pull-up trims shift all corners together and could not fix FLC without breaking FHH, so I kept the single pass device. About 3,400 s of model time remains unused, and no verification feedback is returned after submission.

SPICEFinal submission
* LDO v9: PMOS-input error amp, NMOS common-source gate driver, PMOS pull-up current source, parallel pass devices

XMP1 ND1 VREF TAILP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
